The difference between the monetary value of food and that of a camera.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"1\">B. The difference between a private bank loan and a public charitable foundation.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"2\">C. The difference between a patient&#x27;s gratitude and a reporter&#x27;s hostility to all filming.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"3\">D. Two Chairs Above the Bus Depot","volume":"3","anchor":"chapter-0028","paragraphs":["The inspector arrived shortly before lunch with a yellow folder and a face suggesting that mercy was outside his job description. Mara welcomed him anyway. Mercy House had passed its first electrical test; it had not received permission to open a clinic. The distinction failed to excite the mayor's consulting office, which had already drafted a greeting-card announcement calling the reopening \"an unsurpassed achievement of leadership.\"","\"His office is eager to applaud,\" June said. \"Can it send a plumber?\"","The inspector glanced at the announcement. \"They could start by learning the difference between a cafe and a hospital.\"","Noor was in the old clinic with a consulting physician, checking which services could safely resume. Their plan was modest: appointments, routine follow-up, and support for patients already registered. A full emergency department would entail staffing and equipment they did not have. To overlook that limitation because the building had beds would be a destructive mistake. Mara crossed out a line promising \"comprehensive healthcare\" and wrote a more precise description.","A volunteer protested that the smaller promise sounded dull. Noor asked him whether he preferred an exciting service that failed when a patient needed it. \"A reliable description is beneficial,\" she said. \"Confidence cannot substitute for supervision.\" The volunteer looked at her microscope, then at the empty equipment shelf. He offered to fetch the delivery boxes. It was a wiser contribution than the poster.","The boxes were not there. A purchaser had ordered a replacement refrigerator, but its dispatch notice listed the hospital's old address. The delivery car had gone to an automotive dealership near the airport. Ivo called the driver. \"They have the same street number,\" he explained, \"but fewer patients.\" The driver offered to return after a bus inspection. The delay would necessitate another day's closure of the food service room. Mara felt her motivation leak into the floor.","\"Call out the error,\" Cassian said. \"Do not punish yourself for failing to predict a private sector experiment in geography.\"","She called. The dealership agreed to keep the appliance cool and secure, an undertaking easier to make about a refrigerator than about a stolen duke. Cassian's glance suggested he had heard that thought. He was carrying a cup of tea she had forgotten to drink. \"No charge for delivery,\" he said. She accepted it and accidentally brushed his fingers. The tiny contact did more to arouse her confusion than any formal announcement had managed.","Meanwhile, a chemical smell drifted from the theatre furnace room. Ivo shut the valve and asked everyone to keep clear until the source had been checked. It was a leaking cleaning container, not evidence of a secret experiment. A technician separated the powder from the food-storage bin and arranged a safe disposal. Such a fault might easily have become an accident if the crew had been encouraged to ignore an unfavorable finding merely to meet a deadline.","The inspector made a mark on his sheet. The kitchen could open after the appliance arrived and the cleanup was approved. Fourteen days later he would inspect again. Until then, eight tables, no more. The conditions were strict but flexible enough to allow work. \"A partial reopening,\" Mara said, trying the words. She had wanted everything. Eight tables would feed people while they built something more sound.","Agnes suggested a trial lunch for the residents. The grocer supplied a loaf and some juice at a discount, though the credit account remained frozen. A culinary student offered to develop a wholesome soup. A visiting nurse corrected the seasoning before the cook attempted to compensate with more salt. \"A healthy diet does not have to taste like punishment,\" the nurse said. \"Nor must every kindness arrive as a pill.\"","One patient needed a soft meal; another had trouble with under-nutrition. Their needs were different. Noor would not segregate them from the tables as though illness were an offense. Nor would she pretend the same serving could suffice for all. A consultant reviewed the individual plans. The program was small, practical, and dependent on trained staff. It could soothe a hungry afternoon without being promoted as the terminator of every physiological disorder.","The student laughed at that phrase, then admitted his former employer had used it to sell supplements. A drugstore advertisement made a similarly vague claim. \"They say their product has no side effect,\" he said. \"They have not said what it contains.\" The inspector asked to see the label. The food standards authority would receive the complaint; an allegation about a virus or a germ required a different investigation. One alarming word could not replace a test.","Mara found an older leaflet about Medicare tucked inside a visiting patient's records. Its owner wanted it returned to her American insurance papers, not placed on the clinic noticeboard. A well-run service had to recognize which forms belonged to which person. The smallest intrusion could expose someone's blood test or medical history. Mara sealed the folder and gave it to Agnes, who checked the woman's instructions before putting it away.","Across the yard, Rowan was having a heated argument with his family's senior representative. He had requested permission to release a set of purchasing records. The representative called this insincere idealism; the family could privately make up the shortfall without airing its affairs. Rowan answered straight up that a secret payment would preserve the same leverage. A joint effort required information the residents could use, not favors they had to guess at.","\"You cannot quantify goodwill,\" the representative said.","\"I can quantify an invoice. There are eighty refrigerators on this one. We can find one.\"","That silenced the yard. Rowan passed copies to June and the committee. They showed transactions between three affiliated suppliers, including the theatre scenery company. The book value of equipment had risen without a corresponding increase in deliveries. Such entries were suggestive; they did not reveal where the missing goods had gone. Nevertheless, the contradictory shipping dates deserved a rigorous review. Cassian asked for the originals to be preserved.","The representative warned that publicity might force an auction and destroy the hospital's remaining asset. A court could suspend a sale if it found adequate grounds; Cassian could request that review, not order it. The justice department had already asked for a proper inventory. Mara would have to hand the records over through the appointed officer. \"Damn,\" she whispered, when she saw how much work that would entail. Cassian did not pretend the word was a plan.","Mila made a proxy form for residents unable to attend the committee meeting. Agnes checked its clarity. The vote on temporary management was unanimous among those who participated, although three residents had not authorized anyone to speak for them. June kept that qualification in her report. A unanimous meeting was not proof that every patient approved. A witness to one scene could not be made to represent thousands of people.","The meal began at one. A veteran waiter from a rural town carried soup with a hand steady enough to put Mara's to shame. A woman in a miniskirt and heavy boots offered to wash dishes; the uniformed volunteer initially tried to direct her toward the stage. She reminded him that clothing was a poor guide to competence. He looked visibly embarrassed and apologized. The woman accepted, then handed him an apron. \"You can still improve your rating.\"","A child in an astronaut costume ran past the door and tried to seize a rose from a vase. Cassian saved the vase but let the child keep the fallen bloom. \"Not just an investigator but a gardener,\" Mara said. \"Do you keep doing good deeds when nobody is watching?\" \"I generally break fewer objects then.\" The child informed him that a knight needed a helmet. Gideon supplied a saucepan and was immediately removed from the responsible-adults committee.","After lunch, Mara and Ivo checked the scenery store. An old tourism industry show had left a painted cooling tower, a cardboard yacht, and a cracked drum. The river backdrop came from a seventeenth-century play. Against its faded blossom pattern lay a new metal panel. It was too heavy for scenery. The seam matched the missing equipment cases; an inventory number had been polished away, although ultraviolet light revealed part of the original ink.","Ivo wanted to smash the lock on its case. Mara wanted to go it alone into the rest of the store. Cassian asked them both to stop. Evidence could be lost as easily by enthusiasm as by theft. They photographed the position and requested a technician. The panel might merely be surplus, and that uncertainty was a reason for care. It was not a reason to drag it into the courtyard and call it proof.","At six, Cassian found Mara on a sofa, trying to rub plaster from her wrist. She had a bruise where a cupboard door had struck her. He asked before touching the arm. She let him look. \"You should have told me,\" he said. \"You should not assume I need permission to lift a box.\" His fingers stilled. Her response had been sharper than the question deserved. Both sat with the uncomfortable truth for a moment.","\"I worry,\" he said at last. \"Sometimes I turn that into an instruction before I notice.\"","\"And I hear an instruction before you finish asking.\"","They agreed on something smaller than perfect understanding. If either planned a dangerous action, the other would hear the plan beforehand. Either could object. Neither would secretly replace the other's decision, arrange an unwanted escort, or use affection as a fee for cooperation. \"Unless there is an immediate danger to someone's life,\" Mara added. \"Then we discuss the terrible judgment afterward.\" He laughed, cautiously.","The concert had been postponed because the violinist was ill. Cassian offered an alternative: two chairs on the theatre balcony, sandwiches, and the improbable sunset above the bus depot. She said yes. He asked whether it was still a date without music. She said the absence of an orchestra significantly improved his chance of being heard. They watched workers cross the street. For once, neither had to imitate a bride or a charming guest. They could sip tea and be tired.","\"You make this place feel possible,\" he said.","She looked at him. The compliment was not about a bloodline, a title, or an imagined flawless self. It concerned work she had actually done. She did not know how to answer without making the moment smaller. So she left her hand on the table, palm up. He laid his beside it, waited, and only closed his fingers when she moved hers toward him.","The alarm below them sounded twice. Agnes stood in the courtyard, holding a newly delivered inventory notice. An official vehicle had collected three sealed cases an hour earlier. The authorization carried the hospital's old signature stamp. Nobody on the new committee had approved it. The date ended with their hands releasing each other at the same time. This time, when Mara rose, she told Cassian exactly where she intended to go."],"questions":[{"type":"Qualifier / inference","stem":"Which conclusion about the management vote is supported by the account?","options":["The committee treated silence as permission to vote in favor.","All participants in the vote supported the proposal, but some residents had authorized no representative.","Every resident supported the proposal through either a personal vote or a proxy.","The three residents without representatives opposed the proposal."],"answer":1,"explanation":"unanimous受到among those who participated限定，另有三人未授权他人发言。未授权不等于反对，更不能把沉默算作赞成。","evidence":[20],"shuffled":true}],"wordCount":1839,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0028\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><div class=\"kicker\">3</div><h1>4. data-score=\"27\"></p><button type=\"button\" data-reset=\"27\">清空本章作答，重新自测</button><div class=\"question\" id=\"q-28-1\" data-question=\"27,0\"><p class=\"meta\">1. Qualifier / inference</p><p><strong>Which conclusion about the management vote is supported by the account?</strong></p><div class=\"options\"><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"0\">A. The committee treated silence as permission to vote in favor.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"1\">B. All participants in the vote supported the proposal, but some residents had authorized no representative.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"2\">C. Every resident supported the proposal through either a personal vote or a proxy.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"3\">D. The three residents without representatives opposed the proposal.</button></div><div class=\"feedback\" hidden=\"hidden\"></div></div></section><nav class=\"chapter-nav\" aria-label=\"Chapter navigation\"><button data-go=\"26\">← Previous</button><button data-go=\"28\">Next →</button></nav></article>"},{"id":"v03c05","title":"5. The Goose in the Refrigerator","volume":"3","anchor":"chapter-0029","paragraphs":["At seven the next morning, the kitchen refrigerator began to emit a noise like an offended goose. Mara was sitting on the floor beneath the sink, holding a pipe while Ivo tightened a joint. \"In keeping with the dignity of a public institution,\" she observed. \"Perhaps the goose will chair today's meeting.\" Then the light went out. Worst of all, nobody could agree when it had last been on.","A power interruption had affected three buildings. It did not mean anyone had sabotaged the fridge, although the hospital's former administrator promptly suggested that Mara's management had caused it. He wanted the residents to sign a transfer agreement before breakfast. The agreement would move them to accommodation owned by his firm and allow it to consume most of their rent allowance. He described the offer as supportive. Mara thought the adjective deserved a separate investigation.","Noor inspected the temperature log. A series of missing readings left the food's condition uncertain. She could not use a healthy-looking onion as evidence that everything beside it remained safe. The cook wanted to save the beef; an affordable replacement was not at hand. Noor understood the cost, but considering that some residents were especially vulnerable, she would not gamble their well-being on a smell test. The food had to be held until the appropriate checks were made.","Gideon appeared at the kitchen hatch in a dressing gown. \"If the soup has escaped,\" he said, \"I will testify that it acted alone.\" His humorous tone did not disguise his anxiety. Changes to meals and medication mattered to his diabetes. He had learned to cope with a routine; uncertainty could make an ordinary breakfast difficult. Mara asked what he had already eaten, then fetched Agnes rather than offering medical advice herself.","The administrator waved a brochure beneath her nose. Its cover promised round-the-clock care, optimum nutrition, and an environment that would nurture each resident's independence. An invariable footnote excluded \"pre-existing complexity.\" Mara asked what that meant. \"It is a basic formula,\" he replied. \"A commercial reservation.\" \"You have excluded the people you are offering to take,\" she said. \"You cannot revive a contract by putting a picture of a smiling nurse on it.\"","Cassian checked the transport instructions. Several patients could not ascend a staircase without assistance, let alone carry luggage. The new building had rooms above an inn and no working lift. The firm's description of access was incomplete. Its interpretation of \"near medical services\" included a dentist and a shop selling vitamins. The actual surgeon and neurologist were across the city. Different forms of expertise were being folded into a blur called care.","\"Are you an expert on medicine?\" the administrator demanded.","\"No,\" Cassian said. \"I can read a map.\"","The answer earned a grin from Gideon. Mara almost let her relief get the best of her judgment. A bad destination did not prove Mercy House was safe; their fridge was still silent, and several people still needed food. They ought to do the arithmetic before announcing a victory. She asked Agnes how many meals could come from the local kitchens and what the residents could tolerate.","Noor called the chemist about the medication that required controlled storage. He could hold the affected supply at his pharmacy while they checked the equipment. The physiologist who volunteered on Tuesdays helped assess the day's workload; a therapist rearranged a session so an elderly man did not have to cross the dark stairwell. There was no single generalist qualified to replace them all. Good management meant obtaining the right help, not pretending one heroic person could do every job.","Mara took a tray to the bedroom of a woman who had missed the meeting. She found a bookmark tucked inside the sci-fi bestseller. A cure for every disease, the cover announced. Its promised biomedical formula included a claim about a chromosome that Noor had already challenged. The woman looked ashamed when Mara noticed it. \"Hope is expensive,\" she said. Mara left the book where it was. \"That doesn't make being hopeful a defect. It makes honesty matter more.\"","Downstairs, a crowd had gathered around a food cart. The owner sold soup near the lake and had come with breakfast rolls, a pot of porridge, and a handwritten calorie guide. His daughter, a farmer, had delivered the last milk before dawn. He could not supply the hospital indefinitely. He could spare enough for the morning. Agnes matched each meal to the existing instructions and rejected a sweet refreshment for a patient who needed something different.","\"I can eat almost anything,\" Gideon said.","\"You have already demonstrated that you can complain about almost anything,\" Agnes replied. \"The two findings are discrete.\"","The cart owner asked for payment at cost. Mara counted the money twice. An accountant from Rowan's office would probably have called it a negligible sum. A week of such purchases could radically change a ward budget. Inflationary food prices affected the old and poor disproportionately; a gain on a financial page could mirror a loss on a kitchen shelf. The new committee would publish the bill, not allow sentiment to hide it.","A newspaper reporter had arrived with the former administrator. He asked whether Mara's reluctance to evacuate came from a passion for her new title as the theatre's savior. \"What title?\" she asked. He pointed to a magazine profile. The article called her the city's most sought-after heiress. Its only verified statement was that she wore a coat. \"I would rather talk about the broken power cable,\" she said. \"It is actually involved.\"","June asked to see the reporter's evidence. A photograph showed a crowd outside Mercy House; its subtitle described \"epidemic panic.\" No epidemic had been identified. The reporter admitted that his editor had asked him to heighten the drama. June told him a fearful face did not establish an infectious cause, and a camera should not be used to saturate a story with implications its data could not support. She gave him the time of the power failure and the repair company's number.","The administrator tried to pursue the residents into the ward. Agnes stopped him. They could apply for a visit, she said, but could not bring a sales team into bedrooms without permission. The reporter put his recorder away. It was a small adjustment, yet his questions became less predatory once he had to address people as people. One elderly sister asked whether he would eat the meal he had just photographed. He bought a bowl.","At noon, Ivo found the electrical fault in the shared yard. A contractor had damaged a cable while removing an old cooling unit. The repair order contained the theatre supplier's signature. That connection deserved attention. It did not prove a deliberate attack. A poorly supervised job could bring about the same interruption; calling it sabotage would make their story more exciting while making their investigation less exact.","\"What did you intend to do?\" Cassian asked the contractor, who had come to recover his tools.","\"Remove a dead unit. The parcel order said nobody lived here.\"","Forty-three residents stood within earshot. The man became very interested in his boots. Mara showed him the occupied ward. The faulty order had been issued two weeks earlier, before she obtained investigation rights. Whoever wrote it had treated the building as already empty. They had a dated statement to check, rather than a villain to invent.","Mila helped Ivo install a temporary barrier. Her nylon gloves caught on a metal edge, and the pipe gave her finger a shallow cut. Noor cleaned and dressed it. A bloodied glove could look drastic beside an undamaged hand; photographs made it easy to confuse the appearance of harm with its degree. June took no picture. Mila wanted a plaster and a chance to finish the work. Both requests were granted.","The committee met in the theatre while the light returned. They could afford two more days of emergency meals. After that, the replacement bill would become impossible unless the contractor accepted responsibility for the damaged cable. Rowan called the insurer and produced the repair records. Mara watched him use a merchant's invoice with more effect than the high performer's grand fund proposal. A handy piece of paper was sometimes worth more than an enormous promise.","When she went to fetch her coat, Cassian followed her to the staircase. \"You looked ready to give them the theatre this morning,\" he said. \"For one instant.\" \"And then?\" \"Then I remembered the footnote.\" She leaned against the wall, weary beyond the usefulness of pride. \"I cannot lose this house before it has had a fair start.\" His expression softened. \"You can care about it without being the only person responsible for its survival.\"","She could think of a promise as a debt, but not every debt had to be paid alone. The work would be worth doing even if nobody called her amazing. Perhaps that was what she had been trying to learn. She took his offered hand for the last stair, then released it to carry two bowls. He carried the other two without making the assistance into a rescue.","By dusk, the meals were paid for, the fridge was cooling, and Gideon had revised his opinion of porridge from \"criminal\" to \"secondary offense.\" The public statements had been corrected, though some readers still responded negatively. The house had not become prosperous overnight. What it had acquired was a clearer account of what had happened and a new name on the removal order. Mara placed that name beside the black square. Evidence / causal inference</p><p><strong>What can the investigators reasonably conclude from the damaged cable and the contractor&#x27;s order?</strong></p><div class=\"options\"><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"0\">A. The administrator caused the interruption because he immediately offered new accommodation.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"1\">B. The supplier&#x27;s removal work needs investigation, but deliberate sabotage has not been established.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"2\">C. The contractor&#x27;s admission establishes that the hospital kitchen is generally unsafe.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"3\">D. The supplier intentionally endangered the residents in order to obtain the theatre.</button></div><div class=\"feedback\" hidden=\"hidden\"></div></div></section><nav class=\"chapter-nav\" aria-label=\"Chapter navigation\"><button data-go=\"27\">← Previous</button><button data-go=\"29\">Next →</button></nav></article>"},{"id":"v03c06","title":"6. A Number Is Not a Relationship","volume":"3","anchor":"chapter-0030","paragraphs":["Mila had cut her hair with kitchen scissors before the housing appointment. The result was changeable according to the angle: severe on one side, adventurous on the other. \"I could not afford a haircut and the bus,\" she told Mara. \"The bus looked harder to perform myself.\" Cassian's attempt to suppress a smile failed. He offered no money until she asked whether he had change for the fare. Then he supplied the exact amount.","The hearing was not a trial and would issue no verdict about the missing patients. Its purpose was to decide whether the landlord had correctly calculated Mila's family's arrears. Mara carried the tenancy agreement; Mila carried her mother's written permission to discuss the rent. A medical summary could explain the missed work without exposing the woman's entire history. Their interests did not align automatically, but privacy and a fair hearing were not inherently at odds with each other.","The officer's name was Lady Orsett. She had no aristocratic title; Lady was what her mother had called her. Cassian introduced himself by his full name and received a number in the queue. \"A royal innovation,\" he murmured. \"We have invented equality through waiting.\" Mara sat beside him. A plastic wreath around the reception clock still announced the previous festival. It had outlived three notices about the office's new digital efficiency.","Mila could not tell whether the landlord's representative was contemptuous or merely thirsty. He drank two cups of water before explaining that the bank had lost the corrected records. His argument depended on treating the correction as late, even though the receipt showed it had arrived on time. More often than not, a missing document harmed the party least able to replace it. That did not make every loss deliberate. It did make the completeness of the repository important.","Orsett asked the clerk to retrieve the original file. The screen showed nineteen outstanding cases in the hospital neighborhood. Mara felt a wild hope, then checked herself. Nineteen appeared in more than one place; a repeated number was not a relationship. They would need names and dates before linking rent claims to patient referrals. Numerical coincidence was a poor substitute for evidence, however incredible the connection would make a newspaper story.","The original receipt established when Mila's mother had paid. An extra charge was removed. A repayment schedule would be based on the corrected sum. \"You will be responsible for explaining any further change,\" Orsett told the landlord. \"And you must not force the tenant to pay a charge that the agreement does not authorize.\" Mila's shoulders lowered by a millimeter. It was a small movement, but Mara saw it clearly.","The victory did not kill off the debt. Mila still had to meet the next payment, and her mother still needed time to recover. Nor could a housing officer prescribe a remedy for a heart condition. Distinct problems could interact without becoming a single problem one person could solve. Mara was learning to resist the impulse to promise everything at the instant a person expressed gratitude. A promise made without resources could breed the next disappointment.","Back at Mercy House, Noor was discussing Tomas's referral with a visiting specialist in neurology. His file mentioned an earlier coronavirus infection and a period of pneumonia; it also recorded complaints that did not fit that explanation neatly. A benign initial test did not prove the absence of every illness. An unusual symptom did not automatically establish the most alarming cause. \"We must be determined to find out,\" Noor said, \"rather than determined to confirm what we already expect.\"","Tomas sat with a paper cup, rubbing the edge between his thumb and finger. A vein stood out on his hand. He did not want another lecture about how psychology could affect memory. He wanted to know whether the reference to psychiatry meant that nobody believed him. The specialist explained the scope of the referral. Medical disciplines could work alongside one another; an emotional response did not disqualify a physical complaint, and a physical diagnosis would not make emotional support unnecessary.","\"I suffer from enough confusion without a contest between departments,\" Tomas said.","\"Then we must not add one,\" Noor answered.","She asked his permission before contacting the port clinic. He agreed to a limited request for the appointment records. That distinction would matter later. The hospital could not simply collect every detail of nineteen people's lives because its investigators found a shared route intriguing. A patient could agree to one inquiry and object to another. Neither decision would cost him a meal or a medicine delivery.","While the reply was pending, Agnes brought a snack. Tomas had regained some appetite, though he remained a critical eater. He asked whether the noodle dish was meant to be resistant to human teeth. Agnes replaced it with a soft corn cake and a peach. A nutrient chart hung over the serving hatch. It had been designed for the staff, not as a contest in which a disadvantaged patient earned the right to eat by proving nutritional knowledge.","Mara went to the theatre to inspect the costumes scheduled for sale. Their labels came from a travelling production that had toured America and Asia before returning through the port. A mass-produced jacket from Chicago shared a crate with a silk costume bought in Japan. The fashion industry had apparently discovered a profitable method whereby imaginary princes required more outfits than actual carpenters. Two size zero dresses had been worn by mannequins, not actors. Mila asked whether the mannequins paid rent.","An auction dealer offered to buy the entire lot. His payment would cover the hospital's food bill, but his demand included the building's antique cannon, the children's scenery, and the original plans for its bath complex. \"You overbid for the clothes and underprice everything else,\" Rowan said. The dealer smiled. \"A combination price.\" \"A self-serving one.\" Rowan asked for a separate valuation and accepted the dealer's disapproval as part of the service.","The records of a previous excavation showed a shared storage passage beneath the yard. The carpenter's plan said it had been closed after a water leak. A modern application described the same space as a vacant apartment. Mara asked Ivo to check from the outside before anyone attempted to enter. No invoice was worth being crushed by a wall whose condition remained unknown. An enterprise that relied on making unpaid workers reckless would not be improved merely by charitable motives.","Ivo returned with a sketch. There was no safe access yet. \"A bummer,\" he said. \"The wall is doing an excellent impression of structural confidence.\" He could strengthen it, but the repair required permission and money. Their powerful desire to press forward on the inquiry did not make either resource appear. Rowan put the work on a separate estimate rather than burying it in the kitchen repairs. That separation made the budget less flattering and more honest.","At the committee meeting, the landlord's representative proposed reserving places for patients whose future earnings would repay the investment. He called it a meritocracy. Mrs Bell asked whether a retiree's life had fallen below the market's minimum bid. \"Your rule seems to favor the employable before it has even asked what care is for,\" she said. The representative insisted the policy was practical rather than cruel. Noor replied that a practical design still required an explicit account of whom it excluded.","The visiting specialist had worked in South Carolina and treated people whose insurance differed. Her experience did not authorize her to decide the city's welfare policy. It did help her identify a familiar failure: the patient often became the one person expected to understand every rule while being least able to challenge any of them. \"We should simplify access,\" she said, \"without pretending the needs themselves are simple.\"","June's publisher wanted to describe the meeting as an uprising against capitalism. June refused. A questioning attitude toward one financing model did not establish a position on every economic system. Her report would explain the model, the objections, and what remained undecided. When the editor-in-chief complained about the lack of a spectacular headline, she suggested \"People Read Contract.\" He said it lacked dynamism. She said the people involved had experienced quite enough dynamism already.","A copy of the contractor's training material arrived in the afternoon. One old slide discussed human evolution and misspelled Homo sapiens; another advertised a genetic distinction between \"strong\" and \"weak\" bloodlines. The error alone did not discredit every finding, but the hierarchy was scientifically unsubstantiated by anything the company supplied. Noor would not use body type, ancestry, or a client's nationality to infer mental capacity. Such a fixation could ill-treat people while pretending to classify them.","Cassian photographed the slides. Their circulation had been restricted to staff who attended a convention. The slide deck's author was described as a physicist, but there was no reference to the relevant medical qualifications. Titles were not interchangeable. The brochure's deceptiveness lay partly in allowing genuine achievements in one field to suggest authority in another. Rowan wanted the name preserved so they could check it, not released online beside an accusation they could not yet prove.","At sunset, Tomas brought a child's globe to the kitchen. He had spent an hour explaining the equator and the two hemispheres to an audience interested mainly in whether fish fell off the bottom. The task had been a pastime, not therapy. Now he pointed at the hospital's location. \"My route changed before the missing hours,\" he said. \"I used to cross here. Then the dispatcher sent me along the coast.\"","Mara asked him to draw the change on blank paper. She did not supply a destination or tell him what the black square might mean. The older route passed the clinic. The newer one passed a storage warehouse. That was worth checking independently. A memory could be authentic and still incomplete; honest recollection did not guarantee accurate measurement. Tomas seemed relieved when she said they would verify it against the driving logs.","Mila arrived with the repaired shoe and her mother's medication. \"A good day?\" she asked. Mara looked at the rent schedule, the closed passage, and the sketch of the road. It was a day with fewer errors and more questions. \"By and large,\" she said. Cassian touched the back of her chair as he passed. \"An extravagant assessment. I had expected a full victory.\" \"You may file a complaint.\" He sat beside her. \"I shall need help with the forms.\""],"questions":[{"type":"Qualification / evidence","stem":"Why does Mara restrain her excitement when the screen shows nineteen housing cases?","options":["The corrected rent calculation has made the medical records irrelevant.","The housing officer has prohibited any further investigation into the cases.","A shared number does not establish that the housing claims involve the nineteen referred patients.","She already knows that the housing and medical records refer to entirely separate people."],"answer":2,"explanation":"原文明确说repeated number不是relationship，需进一步核对姓名与日期。答案保留“尚未建立联系”的范围，不能反过来说已经证明无关。","evidence":[5],"shuffled":true}],"wordCount":1751,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0030\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><div class=\"kicker\">3</div><h1>6.
